# MWCC (mwccpsp 3.0.1 build 219, -O4,p -sdatathreshold 0) codegen notes for Hot Shots Tennis: Get a Grip

Verified findings only: each line names the function where it was proven. Matching agents read this first
and append new verified findings (one `cat >> docs/MWCC-NOTES.md` per finding, never rewriting the file).

## Source shape
- The game is C++ with MSL-style containers. Empty variadic stubs are C++ `extern "C" void f(...)` (0x8822c).
- Ghidra often inverts if/else; try both orders (0x17dab8).
- Ghidra drops parentheses on non-associative float chains: `a * b * c` may be `a * (b * c)` (0x17dab8).
- Ghidra prints a signed `x / 2^k` as `if (x < 0) x = x + (2^k-1); ... x >> k`; only `x / 2^k` gives retail's
  bgez/sra/addiu/sra (0xee44c).
- Ghidra hoists repeated subexpressions (index products) into temporaries the source wrote inline (0xee44c).
- Ghidra turns `for (...) { if (hit) { r = i; break; } } return r;` into early returns; the break form
  matches (0x2535f0).
- Struct member access compiles like char-pointer arithmetic, not integer arithmetic: an int-typed base
  with `(base + i*4 + 0x3c)` gives different operand order; `self->items[i]` (or `char *` base with
  `base + 0x3c + i*4`) matches (0x2535f0).
- A global used as a struct base must be a pointer: with an int global, offsets > 0x7fff compile to
  `addiu 0x7fff` + rest (and unaligned swl/swr); retail is lui/addu with a negative offset (0x22c15c, 0x22c4ac).
- Module addresses Ghidra prints as numbers (`(T *)0x34179c`, `FUN(0x30fe20)`) are symbol addresses in
  retail: lui/addiu %hi/%lo, never lui/ori. Use `&DAT_0034179c` (0x1b5594). For `index + 0x338b58`
  write the base first: `(int)&DAT_00338b58 + index` (0xee44c).
- Calls passing a float to a callee without a prototype promote it to double (`__extendsfdf2`); give the
  callee a prototype with `float` parameters (0x97110).

## Frame / stack
- A 16-byte stack slot appears for: an empty allocator class passed by value to a non-inline function; an
  allocator temporary used as `Alloc().allocate(n)`; an address-taken local (e.g. `size` bound to a
  `const&` in a `less<>` functor call). Empty temporaries inside inline functions are optimised away.
- Address-taken locals are laid out in declaration order from the frame top; sorting Ghidra's
  `local_N`/`uStack_N` declarations by N ascending fixes many mismatches.
- `std::max` returning `const&` puts its operands on the stack; `const int&` locals avoid constant folding.
- A by-value char-member struct reaching the callee as `f.v` produces `move v0,zero; andi v0,0xff; seb`.
- Small structs are copied with lwc1/swc1 when passed/returned by value.

## Registers / branches
- `x < y` as the FIRST operand of `||` is computed into a register (`sltu v0/v1; bnez`); written as
  `y > x` or `y <= x` it compiles to `sltu at; bnez at`. A single `if (a < b)` uses `sltu at; beqz at`;
  `if (a >= b)` goes through a register (0xd8c98). A `greater`-style functor `Greater()(n, cap)` instead of
  `Less()(cap, n)` kept the functor's frame slot and fixed the register.
- Branch-likely with a store in the delay slot comes from both paths merging into one store:
  `T *mem = new(...); if (mem) mem = ctor(mem, ...); T *o = mem;` (0x134414); ternary and if/else forms
  stay 2 words off.
- A constructor writing through a local copy of `this` (`L *self = this; self->a = 0; ...`) stops MWCC
  folding `&y.b` into one offset (0x2291cc).

## Pragmas (allowed per file)
- `#pragma opt_strength_reduction off` when retail recomputes `base + i*k` instead of walking a pointer.
- `#pragma inline_depth(8)` for deep inline chains (container code).

## Runtime symbols (in symbols/EBOOT.BIN.txt)
- operator new 0xbcc `__nw__FUi`, delete 0xa00 `__dl__FPv`, delete[] 0x9e4 `__dla__FPv`, `__getreent` 0xfe3c
  (stderr at +0xc), fprintf 0xf3e8, abort 0xd518, length_error thrower 0x43b4, memmove 0x11090
  (`FUN_00011090`), memset 0x114c0 (`FUN_000114c0`).

## Appended findings (newest last)
- 2026-10-15 (0xb49ec family, 44 functions): each EMPTY tag object passed BY VALUE reserves a 4-byte stack
  slot that is never written; slots from tags made in the outer function or one inline level down sit above
  the address-taken locals, tags forwarded one level deeper land below `std::max` temporaries. A parameter
  taken by value (not const&) also gets its own slot. Trick: a named `int pad[k]` local reveals hidden slots
  through the frame-size change. 0xb4d00: placement-new move loops written `--p; --last; new (p) T(*last);`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x1afcd4): Ghidra prints `i <= C` as `i < C+1`; MWCC compiles `i <= 0x179` to `slti at,..,0x17a`
  but `i < 0x17a` to `slti v0` in a loop test. Try the inclusive bound when only the compare register differs.
- 2026-10-15 (0x1140d0, 0x127a68): Ghidra drops arguments of calls whose callee it typed with fewer params
  (base constructors taking `this`, a member object passed in a0 next to a float). Retail's `move sN,aN`
  order and a0 loads before the jal show the real argument list.
- 2026-10-15 (0xdcca0): Ghidra merges consecutive `if (m == 1) {...} if (m == 2) {...}` into
  `if (m == 1) {...; t = m;} else t = m; if (t == 2)`; the source re-reads the member in each test (retail
  has a branch-likely with the reload in its delay slot).
- 2026-10-15 (0x187308): an `int` object parameter used for `param_1 + 0x2928` call arguments computes
  the arguments in a different order than a `char *` (struct) parameter; type such bases as pointers.
- 2026-10-15 (0xdb7f4): globals at odd addresses are bytes (lbu), at 2 mod 4 at most halfwords.
- 2026-10-15 (0x191000): a by-value struct parameter is copied into the callee's frame (lwc1/swc1 block) unless
  its type has a user-defined copy constructor; then MWCC passes it by invisible reference with no callee copy,
  and (unlike a `const T&` parameter) the scheduler knows its loads cannot alias address-taken locals, so
  local stores sink below the field loads. Symptom: retail reads fields straight off a1/a2 but one `sw` sits
  later than ours with `const T&`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x146500): an empty tag temporary passed by value (`f(v, n, Tag())`) compiles to `move v0,zero; seb aN,v0` (no frame slot). `int n = last - first;` is CSE'd into a later `(last - first) * sizeof(T)` (one fewer saved reg); retail's recomputation comes from `int n = ((char *)last - (char *)first) / (int)sizeof(T);`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x126c2c): Ghidra prints a member sub-object null test (destructors: `if (&this->m) ...`)
  as `this != (T *)0xfffffc90`; write `(char *)this + 0x370 != 0` (addiu + beqz in retail). hstgen does it.
- 2026-10-15 (0x164c60): retail's double load of one global (`lw v1,X; lw v0,X` before the branch, one for the compare, one as the return value) comes from an inline clamp taking lo/hi by const reference and returning by value (`v < lo ? lo : (hi < v ? hi : v)`); if/return, ternary and macro forms load once. 0x1646a0 shows the same pattern for float bounds.
- 2026-10-15 (0xbc6c0, 0xff548, 0xfcb7c): destructors' `if (m != 0) { FUN_000104e0(); m = 0; }` — the free
  call takes the tested member (`FUN_000104e0(m)`); Ghidra typed the callee without parameters. hstgen v15.
- 2026-10-15 (0x127870): a 0/1 result Ghidra types `bool` is often an `int` return in retail (no andi 0xff).
- 2026-10-15 (0xfcbe4): Ghidra constant-propagates a defaulted parameter (`if (n == 0) { n = C; t = C*4; }`)
  and prints strength-reduced sizes `(n*4 + n)*4`; the source is `if (n == 0) n = C;` and `n * 0x14 + 0x18`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x179fb0): an unused by-value one-char struct parameter (caller passes `seb a3`) adds no slot on
  its own, but together with one outer-level empty tag (inline helper taking `Tag` by value) it gives the
  never-written slot above `need` AND a 16-byte larger frame; the empty tag alone gives only the slot above.
- (2026-10-15, 0x1167fc 0x148af0 0xb49ec) Stack slots for address-taken / struct objects go top-down in creation order: first everything the function itself declares or creates (any statement), then inlined objects breadth-first: pass 1 inlines the calls written in the body (program order) and creates the temporaries/used by-value struct params in those bodies; calls inside inlined bodies, and calls in arguments of inlined calls, are inlined in pass 2, and so on. Empty-class temporaries keep their slot even when optimised away; unused by-value params of inlined functions get none (a standalone function still homes them). Split-buffer append recipe (src/eboot/vec_append_n_*.cpp): `buf.reserve(Grow(v, size + n - cap))` (need = Grow's by-value param, pass 2), the char flag built one inline level below reserve() so it follows the max() temporaries, max_size() through an allocator temporary, and empty tag temporaries passed from the function to the fill/move helpers (+1 temporary inside each inlined helper) so four slots sit between the buffer and need; the out-of-line-helper variant (need at 64) has just the two tags. Use a marker (an inline chain 6 deep ending in `f(g(7))` with `f(const int&)`) at the end of the function to see where the phantom slots end.
- (2026-10-15, 0x196720) A recursive member whose recursion MWCC inlined two levels deep (accessors out of line at the inner levels) matches as hand-unrolled inline helpers that call the out-of-line accessors and the function itself.
- 2026-10-15 (0x1ce96c): a one-pointer iterator class returned by value is copied through lwc1/swc1 via a stack slot unless the class has user-declared (non-copy) constructors (`It() {}`, `It(T *)`); then the return is a plain `sw reg,0(a0)`. A user copy constructor instead makes it pass by reference. `sltu v1,zero,v0; xori v1,1` on a call result = `!p(x)` with p returning int, used as bool (not1-style wrapper inside remove_copy_if).
- 2026-10-15 (0x1909ec): deque push_back keeps `i = start + size` in a local before the full-check and reassigns it after growing; computing it once after the if makes MWCC reload on both paths.
- 2026-10-15 (0x1a47e8, 0xb13c4): Ghidra splits range checks `lo <= x && x < hi` into if/else-if chains
  that repeat the else body (`if (x < lo) r = 0; else if (x < hi) r = 1; else r = 0;`); only the single
  `&&` condition reproduces retail. `v = obj ? obj->f : -1;` (ternary) for Ghidra's if/else assignment.
  hstperm has m_chain_merge and m_ternary.
- (2026-10-15, 0x11ec54) An empty tag temporary passed by value whose class DERIVES from another empty class (`struct fwd_tag : in_tag {}`; `f(a, b, c, fwd_tag())`) gets a never-written frame slot below the call's by-value argument copies and is passed as `lb aN,slot(sp)`; a non-derived empty tag temporary compiles to `move v0,zero; seb aN,v0`, and a named tag local puts its slot at the frame top instead.
- 2026-10-15 (0xf6850): float field copies: an interleaved lwc1/swc1 pair per field is field-by-field
  assignment; a batch of lwc1 loads then a batch of swc1 stores is a struct assignment (`d->rot = s->rot`).
- (2026-10-15, 0xd71a8) A recursive inline function (tree destroy(node)) is expanded into its own out-of-line copy `inline_depth` levels deep, and the deepest levels call out-of-line copies of tiny accessors/allocator members; written naturally those copies become local weak functions in our object (shadowing the retail addresses), so spell the levels out as static inline helpers whose inner calls go to FUN_ addresses. `#pragma inline_depth(8)` was needed for the fully inlined top level (value dtor -> string dtor).
- (2026-10-15, 0x907fc) Reading a float member through a trivial inline getter (`1.0f / obj->GetScale()`) swaps the div.s source registers compared with `1.0f / obj->scale`. A 64-byte matrix copied with unscheduled `lwc1 $f0 / swc1 $f0` pairs is a user-declared memberwise copy constructor (aliasing blocks scheduling); a by-value inline getter into a named local (`Mat4 m = obj->GetMatrix();`) places the copy where retail has it.
- (2026-10-15, 0x11d190) A run of N identical `beqz this` checks in a destructor = N nested member/base layers whose destructors are implicit (each inlined destructor keeps its own `if (this)`). `delete p` null-checks p only when the pointee has a destructor (an empty user `~T() {}` is enough).
- (2026-10-15, 0x97b2c) The implicit copy assignment of a non-POD class (any user-declared constructor) is memberwise: each array is a count-down block-copy loop (two elements per iteration when the count is even, lb/lh/lbu by element type), and a small struct member loads all its fields before storing. `extern "C" S *f(S *a, const S *b) { *a = *b; return a; }` reproduces the out-of-line operator=.
- (2026-10-15, 0x11dad8) MWCC folds `max - 1` through inline calls, local statics, throw() and const& helpers; retail's unfolded `lui/ori; addiu -1` before a store came from store-then-decrement on the static (`G = A().max_size(); --G;`). In the same function the order of an iterator constructor's arguments and writing ++ through a local (`T *q = ptr + 1; if (q == end) q = beg; ptr = q;`) decided the register allocation, and a shared `return x.ptr < y.ptr;` tail gave retail's `bnez; nop; b; ...` layout.
- (2026-10-15, 0x116424) When a local's destructor has no symbol-map name, drop the destructor from the class and call `FUN_xxx(&obj, -1)` explicitly at the end of the scope: the generated code is identical (no EH difference observed for this split-buffer append).
- 2026-10-15 (0x14f228): a function returning a small struct by value takes the hidden return pointer in a0
  and ends with `move v0,zero`; build the result with a constructor (`return It(n);`) to store straight into
  it. Node-holder register pairing (alloc ref vs node in s4/s5) flips when the value is constructed through
  the raw pointer (`Node *m = a.allocate(1); Holder h(a, m); new (&m->value_) T(x);`) instead of `h.get()`;
  creation order and declaration order of the two do not matter.
- (2026-10-15, 0x103068 0x103128 0x103480) `jal 0x100c` with `addiu t9,sp,N` in the delay slot is a pointer-to-member-function call: 0x100c is MWCC's `__ptmf_scall` (t9 = &ptmf, a0 = object). The PTMF constant is 12 bytes {this_delta, vtbl_offset (-1 = non-virtual), func or vptr offset} emitted in .data (header `data=0x...`, not rodata=); virtual slot k sits at vtbl_offset 8+4k. The source is `for_each(v.begin(), v.end(), mem_fun(&T::f))` with a {p} iterator struct. hstmatch needs `__ptmf_scall = 0x0000100c` in symbols (not there yet; sources parked in wip/).
- (2026-10-15, 0x11c998) For an unsigned if/else on `n < 2`, `if (n < 2) {A} else {B}` puts A first (beqzl + b), `if (n >= 2)`/`if (2 <= n)` compute into a register (sltiu v1; bnezl v1); retail's `sltiu at,..,2; bnezl at,join; <A's single store in delay slot>` with B falling through came from `if (n > 1) {B} else {A}` (also `if (1 < n)`).
- (2026-10-15, 0x115cac) Assigning a 16-byte float struct from a named local filled by an out-pointer call (`Vec4 r; F(&r, &v, s); m = r;`) copies in unscheduled lwc1/swc1 pairs; retail's scheduled copy (4 loads at +4,+8,+12,+0 then 4 stores) comes from an inline operator returning the struct by value (`m = v * s;` with `Vec4 r; F(&r, &v, s); return r;` inside).
- 2026-10-15 (0x11d7e4): an empty destroy walk `for (; first != last; ++first)` over `[data, data + n)` keeps retail's `beq first,last` only when one bound re-reads the member (`self->data_`); with both bounds from one cached local MWCC folds the test to `n == 0` (beqz). 0x14aaa0: in a container destructor `delete data` on an element type with a destructor re-runs the element destructor; retail's bare `beqz a0; jal 0xa00` is `if (data) operator delete(data);`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x299694, family 0x299988): not MWCC. `sw ra,20(sp)` in a 32-byte frame (MWCC saves ra at frame-4), t0-t9 temporaries, movn/movz and `j` between blocks mark GCC (SDK) code even above 0x88000; skip such functions until the psp-gcc kits are available.
- (2026-10-15, 0x1519b4) `beqz v0,join; nop; addiu v0,v0,K` (Ghidra: `g ? g + K : g`) is an implicit derived-to-base pointer conversion (base at offset K, e.g. `struct D : B0, Obj {}` with sizeof(B0) == K); a ternary `g ? (T *)(g + K) : 0` gives `beqzl; move v0,zero` instead. An id-dispatch if/else-if chain whose negative case falls through as `bgezl a1,..; slti; b join; nop` came from an inline helper with early returns (`if (id < 0) return obj;` with obj read before the first test).
- 2026-10-15 (0x1e2990, 0xd1f3c, 0xd5198): `lw t9,0(a0); lw t9,8+4k(t9); jalr t9` is a C++ VIRTUAL call (slot k;
  MWCC vtables have an 8-byte header). C function-pointer calls use v0 instead, so these functions only
  match as C++ (`struct C { virtual void d0(); ... virtual R m(...); }; ((C *)p)->m(...)`), with the function
  itself `extern "C"`. hstgen variants 17/18 emit this.
- 2026-10-15 (0xe99d4, 0xbdac4): slot 0 called with a1 = 1 is the virtual DELETING destructor: the source is
  `delete p;` with `struct C { virtual ~C(); };`. Ghidra shows delete's own null check as an extra
  `if (p == 0) {A} else {dtor(p,1); A}` branch.
- 2026-10-15 (0x19695c, tree insert-at): copying an 8-byte pair into a new node with lw/sw (not lwc1/swc1) needs a member-wise copy constructor on the value type (`V(const V &o) : a(o.a), b(o.b) {}`); retail's extra saved register for the allocator (`addiu s6,s5,4` before `jal 0xbcc`) comes from binding the allocator reference first: `Alloc &a = t->alloc(); Holder h(a, a.allocate(1));`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x11e3e8, deque segment walk): a 16-byte iterator copied with the implicit copy constructor goes through lwc1/swc1 to a stack slot; a member-wise copy constructor lets MWCC keep its fields in registers. Iterators taken BY VALUE (not const&) let MWCC schedule their field loads past stores to address-taken locals (`min` temporaries). Reading a field into a local first (`T **b = x.beg_; int n = x.p_ - y.p_;`) puts that load in the first basic block before the signed-divide branch; `if (a >= b)` tests come out as `sltu t0; bnezl t0`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x179ad8, vector assign(n, x)): an EMPTY tag struct passed by value to an out-of-line function shows as `move v0,zero; seb aN,v0` (a char-member flag struct gives `andi; seb`, a bool member `andi`); every empty tag object an inline function takes by value without forwarding adds one 4-byte frame slot above the address-taken locals. Retail placing the then-branch's tail after the else-branch (`jal; b tail; move a2,v0`) is `if (c) { x = f(); } else { ...; return; } tail(x);`.
- 2026-10-15 (0xe172c): `jal 0x9e4` (operator delete[]) on `p - 16` with no destroy loop is `delete[] p` for an
  element class with a constructor but a trivial destructor (MWCC keeps a 16-byte array cookie); with a
  destructor MWCC calls __destroy_new_array, with POD elements there is no cookie.
- 2026-10-15 (0x118bfc, 0x1799f0, 0xd68ac): retail re-reading a field it just stored into a returned iterator (`lw v1,4(a0)` for `end`) is a mem-initializer reading an earlier member: `Iter(p, d) : ptr(p), beg(d->data_), first(beg + d->start_), end(beg + d->cap_)` (first uses the register, end reloads); `first(d->data_ + ..)` reloads d instead. A fill loop storing straight through the pointer is plain `*p = x`: placement new with an inline `operator new(unsigned, void *)` adds a `beqzl p` per element. An unfolded `lw; subu v1,v1,v1; sw` on a container size is a destructor clearing via `int n = size; shrink(n, Alloc());` (the empty allocator temporary also costs a 16-byte frame slot).
- 2026-10-15 (0x1cd9c0, unmatched remove_if; compile-verified): without `#pragma inline_depth` a call made
  from an inlined function is not inlined (retail's out-of-line iterator `*`/`++`/`!=`/copy at 0x1c8bf8..0x1c8c2c);
  a 4-byte iterator with a template converting ctor is still passed in a register, yet the ternary-return
  copy becomes memberwise lw/sw (a real copy ctor would switch to invisible reference); an 8-byte-aligned
  by-value struct parameter's callee copy goes to the bottom of the frame instead of the top.
- 2026-10-15 (0x14f3cc): list erase(pos) whose a2 iterator is written in place (`sw v1,0(a2)`) and reloaded after the delete call is `It erase(List *l, It pos)` with a user copy constructor on It (by invisible reference, no callee copy, `return pos;` with no `move v0,zero`); it also lets the n->prev load and the final pos load hoist above the pos/size stores. Without the copy constructor the frame and body change completely.
- 2026-10-15 (0x1532e4): with EABI the int and float parameters take independent registers, so `f(void *, Ctx *, float)` and `f(void *, float, Ctx *)` are both a0/a1/f12; only the order of the prologue moves (`mov.s $f20,$f12` vs `move s0,a1`) and of the call-argument setup shows the declared order, for the function and for the virtual callee alike. Factory delete-on-failure `if (c->init(t, ctx)) { if (c) delete c; return 0; } return c;` gives retail's beqzl v0 / beqzl s1 / beqz s1 chain (virtual dtor at slot 0, +8).
- 2026-10-15 (0x11f440, 0x101c18): ternary `cap ? cap - 1 : 1` puts the else value in the beqzl delay slot (`cap == 0 ? 1 : cap - 1` gives bnezl with cap-1 in the slot). `for_each(b, e, bind2nd(mem_fun(&T::f), flag))` with MSL-shaped binder2nd/mem_fun1_t (bool member, ptmf + bool = 16-byte binder at the top, 16-byte copy below the iterator copies) matches out of the box; a NON-virtual member needs its mangled name in symbols (PTMF {0,-1,func}).
- 2026-10-15 (0x11bcb8, 0x120cc8): by-value iterator params with a TRIVIAL copy ctor are copied by the callee into its frame (lwc1/swc1); give the class a user-declared copy ctor (MSL `__iterator(const __iterator<false>&)`) and the callee works in the caller's copy. `std::distance(first, last)` taking iterators by value makes MWCC hoist every field load of the copies; the cdeque iterator difference then matched only with `T *f = x.first;` as its first statement (0x11be88 too).
- 2026-10-15 (0x11be88): `Iter i = f(...)` with a non-inlined sret callee goes through a temporary plus copy unless the struct has a user-declared constructor (e.g. `Iter() {}`), which gives RVO into i. A {int,int,float} element copied by a memberwise copy ctor gives lw/sw,lw/sw,lwc1/swc1; the implicit copy uses lwc1/swc1 for all three words.
- 2026-10-15 (0x120cc8): inline-call/ctor arguments are evaluated right to left. CSE keys on type: begin()'s data+start*4 / data+cap*4 sums were recomputed for end() (loads and shifts still shared) only when end()'s sums were `const T*` (const_iterator); a local copy of data_ does not stop CSE.
- 2026-10-15 (0x11d894): wrap-aware cdeque `operator<` matched retail's branch layout only with ONE shared final `return x.ptr < y.ptr;` (`if (x.ptr >= x.first) { if (y.ptr < y.first) return true; } else { if (y.ptr >= y.first) return false; } return x.ptr < y.ptr;`): the likely-branch delay slot is filled from that shared block.
- 2026-10-15 (0x11e790, not matched): frame slots follow code order, except temporaries created inside an inlined body (e.g. an inline ctor's argument temporaries) go below every caller-level local/temporary. Retail calls the iterator copy ctor out of line (0x11ecdc) for by-value args of the out-of-line insert, yet inlines the copy in `end() - 1`.
- 2026-10-15 (0x19660c, tree find-insert-position): two pointer locals live across a loop got their saved registers in reverse declaration order; retail's `lw s0,root; addiu s1,a0,4` needed the parent (`Node *y = &t->header_;`) declared BEFORE the cursor (`Node *x = root;`), with the `j = 0;` store kept first. A plain `char` global flag gives `lb`; retail's `lbu` is `bool` (0x16a508).
- 2026-10-15 (0x2934ec): Ghidra structures consecutive null-checked blocks as `if (p == 0) { t = m2; } else
  { act(p); m1 = 0; t = m2; }` (the next statement copied into both branches). The source is
